editor_ui_property_label :: proc(rect: Rect, label: string) -> (remaining: Rect) {
	if label == "" {
		remaining = rect
		return
	}

	l, h := split_rect_left(rect, EDITOR_PROPERTY_LABEL_WIDTH, 0)
	editor_ui_label(label, l, .Right)
	_, remaining = split_rect_left(h, EDITOR_PROPERTY_LABEL_MARGIN, 0)
	return
}

editor_ui_text_size :: proc(s: string) -> Vec2 {
	return {}//rl.MeasureTextEx(editor_font, temp_cstring(s), EDITOR_TEXT_SIZE, 0)
}


================================================
File: game/entity.odin
================================================
package game

import "core:fmt"
import k2 "proj:karl2d"

_ :: fmt

Entity_Type :: enum {
	None,
	Player,
	Player_Body,
	Player_Head,
	Button,
	Door,
}

Entity_Type_Data :: union {
	Player_Data,
	Button_Data,
	Door_Data,
}

Entity_Id :: distinct u64

Entity_Handle :: distinct Handle

ENTITY_NONE :: Entity_Handle {}

Entity :: struct {
	id: Entity_Id,
	handle: Entity_Handle,
	type: Entity_Type,
	pos: Vec2,
	vel: Vec2,
	rot: f32,
	origin: Vec2,
	anim: Animation,
	tex: Texture_Name,
	facing: Facing,
	last_grounded_at: f64,
	grounded_time: f32,
	collider: Rect,
	data: Entity_Type_Data,
}

entity_create_from_type :: proc(et: Entity_Type, pos: Vec2) -> Entity {
	e: Entity

	switch et {
	case .None:
	case .Player: 
		e = {
			anim = animation_create(.Player),
			origin = animation_bottom_middle(.Player) + { 0, 1 },
			data = Player_Data {},
		}

		player_set_state(&e, Player_State_Default{})
	case .Player_Body: 
	case .Player_Head:
	case .Button:
		e = {
			anim = animation_create(.Button_Pressable),
			collider = { 8, 0, 8, 16 },
		}
	case .Door:
		e = {
			tex = .Door,
			collider = { 0, 0, 8, 16},
		}
	}

	e.pos = pos
	e.type = et

	return e
}

entity_graphics_offset :: proc(e: Entity) -> Vec2 {
	o: Vec2

	if e.anim.current_frame != .None {
		t := atlas_textures[e.anim.current_frame]
		o = vec2_from_vec2i(t.offset)
	} else {
		t := atlas_textures[e.tex]
		o = vec2_from_vec2i(t.offset)
	}

	if e.facing == .West {
		o.x *= -1
	}

	return o
}

entity_blend_pos :: proc(e: Entity) -> Vec2 {
	pos := e.pos

	if prev, ok := get_entity(prev_world, e.handle); ok {
		pos = frame_lerp(prev.pos, pos)
	}

	return pos
}

entity_blend_rot :: proc(e: Entity) -> f32 {
	if prev, ok := get_entity(prev_world, e.handle); ok {
		return frame_lerp(prev.rot, e.rot)
	}

	return e.rot
}

entity_draw :: proc(e: Entity) {
	if e.anim.current_frame == .None && e.tex == .None {
		return
	}

	texture: Atlas_Texture

	if e.anim.current_frame != .None {
		texture = atlas_textures[e.anim.current_frame]
	} else {
		texture = atlas_textures[e.tex]
	}

	atlas_rect := texture.rect
	dest_rect := rect_from_pos_size(entity_blend_pos(e) + vec2_from_vec2i(texture.offset), {atlas_rect.w, atlas_rect.h})
	
	origin := e.origin
	if e.facing == .West {
		origin.x = dest_rect.w - origin.x
	}

	atlas_rect.w *= dir_from_facing(e.facing).x
	k2.draw_texture_ex(atlas, atlas_rect, dest_rect, origin, entity_blend_rot(e))
}

entity_on_collision :: proc(e: ^Entity, other: ^Entity) {
	switch e.type {
	case .None:
	case .Player:
	case .Player_Body:
	case .Player_Head:
	case .Button:
		button_press(e)
	case .Door:
		
	}
}

entity_tick_physics_SLOW :: proc(e: ^Entity, layers: bit_set[Collision_Layer]) -> (collision: bool, grounded: bool) {
	// y part
	{
		e.pos.y += e.vel.y * dt
		col := entity_collider(e^)
		coll_iter := ha_make_iter(&world.colliders)

		for c in ha_iter_ptr(&coll_iter) {
			m := c.layers & layers

			if m == {} {
				continue
			}

			r := c.rect

			if ce := get_entity_ptr(world, c.entity); ce != nil {
				r = rect_add_pos(ce.collider, ce.pos)
			}

			overlap := rect_intersect(col, r)

			if overlap.h != 0 {
				sign: f32 = (col.y + col.h / 2) < (r.y + r.h / 2) ? -1 : 1
				e.pos.y += overlap.h * sign
				e.vel.y = 0
				grounded = true
				collision = true
				col = entity_collider(e^)
			}
		}
	}

	// x part
	{
		e.pos.x += e.vel.x * dt
		col := entity_collider(e^)

		coll_iter := ha_make_iter(&world.colliders)

		for c in ha_iter_ptr(&coll_iter) {
			m := c.layers & layers

			if m == {} {
				continue
			}

			r := c.rect

			if ce := get_entity_ptr(world, c.entity); ce != nil {
				r = rect_add_pos(ce.collider, ce.pos)
			}

			overlap := rect_intersect(col, r)

			if overlap.w != 0 {
				sign: f32 = (col.x + col.w / 2) < (r.x + r.w / 2) ? -1 : 1
				e.pos.x += overlap.w * sign
				e.vel.x = 0
				collision = true
				col = entity_collider(e^)
			}
		}
	}

	return
}

entity_collider :: proc(e: Entity) -> Rect {
	return rect_add_pos(e.collider, e.pos-e.origin)
}

Facing :: enum {
	East,
	West,
}

dir_from_facing :: proc(f: Facing) -> Vec2 {
	return { f == .East ? 1 : -1, 0}
}


// Code below this line will probably be removed
Door_Data :: struct {
	open: bool,
	open_start: f64,
	open_start_pos: Vec2,
	progress: f32,
}

door_data :: proc(e: ^Entity) -> ^Door_Data {
	assert(e.type == .Door)

	if d, ok := &e.data.(Door_Data); ok {
		return d
	}

	e.data = Door_Data{}
	return &e.data.(Door_Data)
}

door_open :: proc(e: ^Entity) {
	d := door_data(e)
	d.open = true
	d.open_start_pos = e.pos
	d.open_start = time_since_start
}

door_update :: proc(e: ^Entity) {
	d := door_data(e)

	if d.open {
		t := remap(time_since_start, d.open_start, d.open_start + 0.5, 0, 1)
		e.pos.y = lerp(d.open_start_pos.y, d.open_start_pos.y + 16, f32(t))
	}
}

================================================
File: game/game.odin
================================================
package game

import "core:c/libc"
import "core:math"
import "core:os"
import k2 "proj:karl2d"
import vmem "core:mem/virtual"
import "core:hash"
import "base:intrinsics"
import "core:time"

PIXEL_WINDOW_HEIGHT :: 180
TILESET_WIDTH :: 10

Game_Memory :: struct {	
	world: World,
	controlled_entity: Entity_Handle,
	atlas: k2.Texture,
	accumulated_time: f64,

	camera_pos_y: f32,
	camera_pos_y_target: f32,
	camera_pos_x_offset: f32,

	editing: bool,
	editor_state: Editor_State,

	debug_draw: bool,
	auto_reload: bool,

	prev: Game_Memory_Previous,
	prev_arena: vmem.Arena,

	kl_state: ^k2.State,

	last_frame_time: time.Time,
	time_since_start: f64,
}

// Some of the game memory from previous tick, used for interpolation
Game_Memory_Previous :: struct {
	world: World,
	camera_pos_y: f32,
	camera_pos_y_target: f32,
	camera_pos_x_offset: f32,
}

@(private="file")
g_mem: ^Game_Memory

// These are globals that gameplay code in any file can use
prev_world: ^World
world: ^World
time_since_start: f64
atlas: k2.Texture
input: Input

set_camera_pos_x_offset :: proc(o: f32) {
	g_mem.camera_pos_x_offset = o
}

set_controlled_entity :: proc(e: Entity_Handle) {
	g_mem.controlled_entity = e
}

game_camera_target :: proc() -> Vec2 {
	c := ha_get(world.entities, g_mem.controlled_entity)

	target := Vec2 {c.pos.x + g_mem.camera_pos_x_offset, g_mem.camera_pos_y}

	if c_prev, ok := get_entity(prev_world, g_mem.controlled_entity); ok {
		target_prev := Vec2 {c_prev.pos.x + g_mem.prev.camera_pos_x_offset, g_mem.prev.camera_pos_y}
		target = math.lerp(target_prev, target, frame_blend)
	}

	return target
}

game_camera :: proc() -> k2.Camera {
	w := f32(k2.get_screen_width())
	h := f32(k2.get_screen_height())

	return {
		zoom = h/PIXEL_WINDOW_HEIGHT,
		target = game_camera_target(),
		origin = {w/2, h/2},
	}
}

tick :: proc() {
	ent_iter := ha_make_iter(&world.entities)
	for e, h in ha_iter_ptr(&ent_iter) {
		is_controlled := h == g_mem.controlled_entity
		update_animation := true
		#partial switch e.type {
		case .Player:
			if is_controlled {
				player_tick(e)
			}
			update_animation = false

		case .Player_Head:
			if is_controlled {
				player_head_tick(e)
			}

		case .Button:
			button_update(e)
			update_animation = false

		case .Door:
			door_update(e)
		}

		if is_controlled {
			p := e.pos

			player_to_camera_y := p.y - g_mem.camera_pos_y
			if player_to_camera_y > PIXEL_WINDOW_HEIGHT/4 || player_to_camera_y < -PIXEL_WINDOW_HEIGHT/4/* || p.grounded_time > 1*/ {
				g_mem.camera_pos_y_target = p.y
			}

			camera_pos_to_target := g_mem.camera_pos_y_target - g_mem.camera_pos_y
			if math.abs(camera_pos_to_target) > 1 {
				g_mem.camera_pos_y += camera_pos_to_target * dt
			}
		}

		if update_animation && animation_valid(e.anim) {
			animation_update(&e.anim, dt)
		}
	}

	ha_commit_new(&world.entities)
	ha_commit_new(&world.colliders)
}

time_since :: proc(t: f64) -> f64 {
	return g_mem.time_since_start - t
}

full_screen_rect :: proc() -> Rect {
	return {
		0,
		0,
		f32(k2.get_screen_width()),
		f32(k2.get_screen_height()),
	}
}

draw :: proc(cam: k2.Camera) {
	k2.clear({ 41, 61, 49, 255 })
	k2.set_camera(cam)

	k2.draw_texture_rect(atlas, atlas_textures[.Bush].rect, {20, 94})

	ent_iter := ha_make_iter(&world.entities)
	for e in ha_iter(&ent_iter) {
		#partial switch e.type {
			case .Player: player_draw(e)
			case: entity_draw(e)
		}

		if g_mem.debug_draw {
			c := entity_collider(e)
			k2.draw_rect(c, COLOR_DEBUG_RED)
			k2.draw_circle(e.pos, 1, COLOR_DEBUG_YELLOW)
		}
	}

	for t in world.tiles {
		tile_draw(t)
	}

	if g_mem.debug_draw {
		for dds in debug_draw_shapes {
			switch s in dds {
			case Debug_Draw_Rectangle:
				k2.draw_rect(s.rect, s.color)

			case Debug_Draw_Cirlce:
				k2.draw_circle(s.center, s.radius, s.color)
			}
		}
	}

	if g_mem.auto_reload {
		k2.draw_text("Auto Reload", { 20, f32(k2.get_screen_height()) - 40 }, 30, k2.RED)
	}
}

dt: f32

DT_FIXED :: 1.0/60.0

// a value in range [0, 1] that says how much state to take from previous vs current frame
frame_blend: f32

frame_lerp :: proc "contextless" (a, b: $T) -> (x: T) {
	return math.lerp(a, b, frame_blend)
}

update_globals :: proc() {
	time_since_start = g_mem.time_since_start
	atlas = g_mem.atlas
	world = &g_mem.world
	prev_world = &g_mem.prev.world
}


@(export)
game_update :: proc() -> bool {
	update_globals()
	debug_draw_reset(g_mem.debug_draw)
	k2.process_events()

	if g_mem.auto_reload {
		for &f in file_versions {
			if mod, mod_err := os.last_write_time_by_name(f.path); mod_err == os.ERROR_NONE {
				ATLAS_METADATA_HASH :: #load_hash("atlas.odin", "murmur64")
				if mod != f.modification_time {
					reload_error := libc.system("odin run atlas_builder")

					if reload_error == 0 {
						atlas_contents, atlas_contents_ok := os.read_entire_file("game/atlas.odin", context.temp_allocator)

						if atlas_contents_ok && hash.murmur64a(atlas_contents) == ATLAS_METADATA_HASH {
							f.modification_time = mod
							k2.destroy_texture(g_mem.atlas)

							g_mem.atlas = k2.load_texture_from_file(TEXTURE_ATLAS_FILENAME)
							return true
						} else {
							reload_error = libc.system("build_hot_reload.bat")

							if reload_error == 0 {
								return true
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	if k2.key_went_down(.F1) {
		g_mem.auto_reload = !g_mem.auto_reload
	}

	if k2.key_went_down(.F2) {
		if g_mem.editing {
			old_player_pos := PLAYER_START_POS
			old_player_type := Entity_Type.Player

			if p, ok := get_entity(world, g_mem.controlled_entity); ok {
				old_player_pos = p.pos
				old_player_type = p.type
			}

			delete_world(world^)
			editor_wd := editor_clone_state_to_world_data(context.temp_allocator)
			world^ = world_from_world_data(editor_wd)
			g_mem.controlled_entity = find_entity_handle_by_type(world, .Player)

			if p := find_entity_by_type(world, old_player_type); p != nil {
				p.pos = old_player_pos
				g_mem.controlled_entity = p.handle
			}
			editor_shutdown()
		} else {
			editor_init(screen_size(), game_camera_target())
		}

		g_mem.editing = !g_mem.editing
	}

	if k2.key_went_down(.F3) {
		g_mem.debug_draw = !g_mem.debug_draw
	}

	if g_mem.editing {
		//k2.begin_drawing()
		dt = DT_FIXED
		editor_update()
		//k2.end_drawing()
		k2.present()
		return !k2.shutdown_wanted()
	}

	now := time.now()
	g_mem.accumulated_time += time.duration_seconds(time.diff(g_mem.last_frame_time, now))
	g_mem.last_frame_time = now

	old_input := input
	input = get_input()

	// We may miss button presses if we have frames where g_mem.accumulated_time > TIMESTEP. So transfer over things here
	input.jump ||= old_input.jump
	input.separate ||= old_input.separate
	input.nose ||= old_input.nose

	dt = DT_FIXED

	if g_mem.accumulated_time > DT_FIXED {
		vmem.arena_free_all(&g_mem.prev_arena)
		g_mem_prev_arena_alloc := vmem.arena_allocator(&g_mem.prev_arena)

		g_mem.prev = {
			world = clone_world(g_mem.world, g_mem_prev_arena_alloc),
			camera_pos_y = g_mem.camera_pos_y,
			camera_pos_y_target = g_mem.camera_pos_y_target,
			camera_pos_x_offset = g_mem.camera_pos_x_offset,
		}

		for g_mem.accumulated_time > DT_FIXED {
			tick()
			g_mem.time_since_start += DT_FIXED
			time_since_start = g_mem.time_since_start
			g_mem.accumulated_time -= DT_FIXED
		}

		input = {}
	}

	ha_commit_new(&world.entities)
	ha_commit_new(&world.colliders)
	frame_blend = f32(g_mem.accumulated_time/DT_FIXED)

	draw(game_camera())
	k2.present()

	return !k2.shutdown_wanted()
}

screen_size :: proc() -> Vec2 {
	return {
		f32(k2.get_screen_width()),
		f32(k2.get_screen_height()),
	}
}

texture_middle :: proc(name: Texture_Name) -> Vec2 {
	t := atlas_textures[name]
	return {f32(t.document_size.x/2), f32(t.document_size.y/2)}
}

texture_bottom_middle :: proc(name: Texture_Name) -> Vec2 {
	t := atlas_textures[name]
	return {f32(t.document_size.x/2), f32(t.document_size.y)}
}

	kl_state = k2.init(1920, 1080, "Metroidvaniaish")
	k2.set_window_position(200, 200)
}

game_shutdown :: proc() {
	if g_mem.editing {
		editor_shutdown()
	}

	vmem.arena_destroy(&g_mem.prev_arena)
	//unload_atlased_font(g_mem.editor_font)

}

	k2.shutdown()
}

	return g_mem
}

@(export)

	return size_of(Game_Memory)
}

@(export)
game_hot_reloaded :: proc(mem: rawptr) {
	g_mem = (^Game_Memory)(mem)
	k2.set_internal_state(g_mem.kl_state)
	k2.destroy_texture(g_mem.atlas)
	g_mem.atlas = k2.load_texture_from_file(TEXTURE_ATLAS_FILENAME)
	editor_set_globals(&g_mem.editor_state)
	update_globals()
}

@(export)
game_force_reload :: proc() -> bool {
	return k2.key_went_down(.F5)
}

@(export)
game_force_restart :: proc() -> bool {
	return k2.key_went_down(.F6)
}


================================================
File: game/handle_array.odin
================================================
package game

import "core:fmt"
import "core:mem"
import "core:slice"
import vmem "core:mem/virtual"

_ :: fmt

Handle :: struct {
	idx: u32,
	gen: u32,
}

HANDLE_NONE :: Handle {}

Handle_Array :: struct($T: typeid, $HT: typeid) {
	items: [dynamic]T,
	unused_items: [dynamic]u32,
	allocator: mem.Allocator,

	// To make sure we do not invalidate `items` in the middle of a frame, while there are pointers
	// to things it, we add new items to this, and the new items are allocated using the growing
	// virtual arena `new_items_arena`. Run `ha_commit_new` once in a while to move things from
	new_items: [dynamic]^T,
	new_items_arena: vmem.Arena,
}

ha_delete :: proc(ha: Handle_Array($T, $HT), loc := #caller_location) {
	delete(ha.items, loc)
	delete(ha.unused_items, loc)
}

ha_clear :: proc(ha: ^Handle_Array($T, $HT), loc := #caller_location) {
	clear(&ha.items)
	clear(&ha.unused_items)
	clear(&ha.new_items)
}

// Call this at a safe space when there are no pointers in flight. It will move things from
// new_items into items, potentially making it grow. Those new items live on a growing virtual
// memory arena until this is called.
ha_commit_new :: proc(ha: ^Handle_Array($T, $HT), loc := #caller_location) {
	if len(ha.items) == 0 {
		// Dummy item at idx zero
		append(&ha.items, T{})
	}

	for ni in ha.new_items {
		if ni == nil {
			// We must add these, if we don't the indices get out of order with regards to handles
			// we have handed out. We'll just add them as empty objects and then put the index into
			// unused items.
			unused_item_idx := len(ha.items)
			append(&ha.items, T {
				handle = {
					gen = 1,
				},
			})

			append(&ha.unused_items, u32(unused_item_idx))
			continue
		}

		append(&ha.items, ni^)
	}

	vmem.arena_free_all(&ha.new_items_arena)
	ha.new_items = {}
}

ha_clone :: proc(ha: Handle_Array($T, $HT), allocator := context.allocator, loc := #caller_location) -> Handle_Array(T, HT) {
	return Handle_Array(T, HT) {
		items = slice.clone_to_dynamic(ha.items[:], allocator, loc),
		unused_items = slice.clone_to_dynamic(ha.unused_items[:], allocator, loc),
		allocator = allocator,
	}
}

ha_add :: proc(ha: ^Handle_Array($T, $HT), v: T, loc := #caller_location) -> HT {
	if ha.items == nil {
		if ha.allocator == {} {
			ha.allocator = context.allocator
		}

		ha.items = make([dynamic]T, ha.allocator, loc)
		ha.unused_items = make([dynamic]u32, ha.allocator, loc)
	}

	v := v

	if len(ha.unused_items) > 0 {
		reuse_idx := pop(&ha.unused_items)
		reused := &ha.items[reuse_idx]
		h := reused.handle
		reused^ = v
		reused.handle.idx = u32(reuse_idx)
		reused.handle.gen = h.gen + 1
		return reused.handle
	}

	if len(ha.items) == 0 {
		// Dummy item at idx zero
		append(&ha.items, T{})
	}

	new_items_allocator := vmem.arena_allocator(&ha.new_items_arena)
	new_item := new(T, new_items_allocator)
	new_item^ = v
	new_item.handle.idx = u32(len(ha.items) + len(ha.new_items))
	new_item.handle.gen = 1

	if ha.new_items == nil {
		ha.new_items = make([dynamic]^T, new_items_allocator)
	}

	append(&ha.new_items, new_item)
	return new_item.handle
}

ha_get :: proc(ha: Handle_Array($T, $HT), h: HT) -> (T, bool) #optional_ok {
	if ptr := ha_get_ptr(ha, h); ptr != nil {
		return ptr^, true
	}

	return {}, false
}

ha_get_ptr :: proc(ha: Handle_Array($T, $HT), h: HT) -> ^T {
	if h.idx == 0 || h.idx < 0 {
		return nil
	}

	if int(h.idx) >= len(ha.items) {
		// The item we look for might be in `new_items`, so look in there too
		new_idx := h.idx - u32(len(ha.items))
		
		if new_idx >= u32(len(ha.new_items)) {
			return nil
		}

		if item := ha.new_items[new_idx]; item != nil && item.handle == h {
			return item
		}

		return nil
	}

	if item := &ha.items[h.idx]; item.handle == h {
		return item
	}

	return nil
}

ha_remove :: proc(ha: ^Handle_Array($T, $HT), h: HT) {
	if h.idx == 0 || h.idx < 0 {
		return
	}

	if int(h.idx) >= len(ha.items) {
		new_idx := h.idx - u32(len(ha.items))
		
		if new_idx < u32(len(ha.new_items)) {
			// This stops this item from being added during `ha_commit_new`
			ha.new_items[new_idx] = nil
		}

		return
	}

	if item := &ha.items[h.idx]; item.handle == h {
		append(&ha.unused_items, h.idx)

		// This makes the item invalid. We'll set the index back if the slot is reused.
		item.handle.idx = 0
	}
}

ha_valid :: proc(ha: Handle_Array($T, $HT), h: HT) -> bool {
	return ha_get_ptr(ha, h) != nil
}

Handle_Array_Iter :: struct($T: typeid, $HT: typeid) {
	ha: ^Handle_Array(T, HT),
	index: int,
}

ha_make_iter :: proc(ha: ^Handle_Array($T, $HT)) -> Handle_Array_Iter(T, HT) {
	return { ha = ha }
}

ha_iter :: proc(it: ^Handle_Array_Iter($T, $HT)) -> (val: T, h: HT, cond: bool) {
	val_ptr: ^T

	val_ptr, h, cond = ha_iter_ptr(it)

	if val_ptr != nil {
		val = val_ptr^
	}

	return
}

ha_iter_ptr :: proc(it: ^Handle_Array_Iter($T, $HT)) -> (val: ^T, h: HT, cond: bool) {
	cond = it.index < len(it.ha.items) + len(it.ha.new_items)

	for ; cond; cond = it.index < len(it.ha.items) + len(it.ha.new_items) {
		// Handle items in new_items
		if it.index >= len(it.ha.items) {
			idx := it.index - len(it.ha.items)

			if it.ha.new_items[idx] == nil {
				it.index += 1
				continue
			}

			val = it.ha.new_items[idx]
			h = val.handle
			it.index += 1
			break
		}

		if it.ha.items[it.index].handle.idx == 0 {
			it.index += 1
			continue
		}

		val = &it.ha.items[it.index]
		h = val.handle
		it.index += 1
		break
	}

	return
}

// Test handle array and basic usage documentation.

ha_test :: proc() {
	Ha_Test_Entity :: struct {
		handle: Ha_Test_Entity_Handle,
		pos: [2]f32,
		vel: [2]f32,
	}

	Ha_Test_Entity_Handle :: distinct Handle

	ha: Handle_Array(Ha_Test_Entity, Ha_Test_Entity_Handle)

	h1 := ha_add(&ha, Ha_Test_Entity {pos = {1, 2}})
	h2 := ha_add(&ha, Ha_Test_Entity {pos = {2, 2}})
	h3 := ha_add(&ha, Ha_Test_Entity {pos = {3, 2}})

	ha_remove(&ha, h2)

	// This one will reuse the slot h2 had
	h4 := ha_add(&ha, Ha_Test_Entity {pos = {4, 2}})
	assert(h4.idx == 4)

	assert(h1.idx == 1)
	assert(h2.idx == 2)
	assert(h3.idx == 3)
	assert(h1.gen == 1)
	assert(h2.gen == 1)
	assert(h3.gen == 1)

	if _, ok := ha_get(ha, h2); ok {
		panic("h2 should not be valid")
	}

	if h4_ptr := ha_get_ptr(ha, h4); h4_ptr != nil {
		assert(h4_ptr.pos == {4, 2})
		h4_ptr.pos = {5, 2}
	} else {
		panic("h4 should be valid")
	}

	if h4_val, ok := ha_get(ha, h4); ok {
		assert(h4_val.pos == {5, 2})
	} else {
		panic("h4 should be valid")
	}

	// This call moves new items from new_items into items. Needs to be run for example at
	// end of frame in a game.
	ha_commit_new(&ha)

	if h4_val, ok := ha_get(ha, h4); ok {
		assert(h4_val.pos == {5, 2})
	} else {
		panic("h4 should be valid")
	}

	ha_remove(&ha, h4)
	h5 := ha_add(&ha, Ha_Test_Entity {pos = {6, 2}})
	assert(h5.idx == 4)

	ha_delete(ha)
}

// generic odin helpers

package game

import "core:strings"
import "base:intrinsics"

increase_or_wrap_enum :: proc(e: $T) -> T {
	ei := int(e) + 1

	if ei >= len(T) {
		ei = 0
	}

	return T(ei)
}

temp_cstring :: proc(s: string) -> cstring {
	return strings.clone_to_cstring(s, context.temp_allocator)
}

// There is a remap in core:math but it doesn't clamp in the new range, which I
// always want.
remap :: proc "contextless" (old_value, old_min, old_max, new_min, new_max: $T) -> (x: T) where intrinsics.type_is_numeric(T), !intrinsics.type_is_array(T) {
	old_range := old_max - old_min
	new_range := new_max - new_min
	if old_range == 0 {
		return new_range / 2
	}
	return clamp(((old_value - old_min) / old_range) * new_range + new_min, new_min, new_max)
}

is_valid_enum :: proc(v: $T) -> bool where intrinsics.type_is_enum(T) {
	return v >= min(T) && v <= max(T)
}

File: game/input.odin

package game

import k2 "proj:karl2d"

Input :: struct {
	movement_x: f32,
	jump: bool,
	separate: bool,
	nose: bool,
}

// don't use this directly, rather use the global `input` struct that works
// correctly when ticking several frames at once.
get_input :: proc() -> Input {
	input: Input

	if k2.key_is_held(.Left) {
		input.movement_x -= 1
	}

	if k2.key_is_held(.Right) {
		input.movement_x += 1
	}

	if k2.key_went_down(.Space) {
		input.jump = true
	}

	if k2.key_went_down(.A) {
		input.separate = true
	}

	if k2.key_went_down(.S) {
		input.nose = true
	}

	return input
}

	return { f32(p.x), f32(p.y) }

================================================
File: game/player.odin

package game

import k2 "proj:karl2d"
import "core:math"
import "core:math/linalg"

Player_State_Default :: struct {

}

Player_State_Head_Detatching :: struct {

}

Player_State_Nose_Extending :: struct {
	tip_pos: Vec2,
}

Player_State_Nose_Retracting :: struct {
	tip_pos: Vec2,
}

Player_State_Nose_Retracting_Pull :: struct {
	tip_pos: Vec2,
}

Player_State :: union #no_nil {
	Player_State_Default,
	Player_State_Head_Detatching,
	Player_State_Nose_Extending,
	Player_State_Nose_Retracting,
	Player_State_Nose_Retracting_Pull,
}

Player_Data :: struct {
	state: Player_State,
	state_start_time: f64,
}

player_set_state :: proc(p: ^Entity, s: Player_State) {
	d := &p.data.(Player_Data)
	d.state = s
	d.state_start_time = time_since_start
	p.collider = {1, 1, 8, 16}

	switch &s in d.state {
	case Player_State_Default:
		p.anim = animation_create(.Player)
		p.origin = animation_bottom_middle(.Player) - {0, 1}

	case Player_State_Head_Detatching:
		p.collider = {}
		p.anim = animation_create(.Player_Detach_Head)
		p.origin = {5, 25}

	case Player_State_Nose_Extending:
		p.anim = animation_create(.Player)
		p.origin = animation_bottom_middle(.Player) - {0, 1}
		s.tip_pos = player_mouth_pos(p^)

	case Player_State_Nose_Retracting:
		p.anim = animation_create(.Player)
		p.origin = animation_bottom_middle(.Player) - {0, 1}

	case Player_State_Nose_Retracting_Pull:
		p.anim = animation_create(.Player)
		p.origin = animation_bottom_middle(.Player) - {0, 1}
	}
}

player_tick :: proc(p: ^Entity) {
	d := &p.data.(Player_Data)
	state_elapsed := time_since(d.state_start_time)

	switch &s in d.state {
	case Player_State_Default:
		if input.movement_x != 0 {
			animation_update(&p.anim, dt)
		}

		if input.movement_x > 0 {
			p.facing = .East
		} else if input.movement_x < 0 {
			p.facing = .West
		}

		p.vel.x = input.movement_x * 80
		p.vel.y += 1000 * dt
		_, grounded := entity_tick_physics_SLOW(p, { .Player })

		if grounded {
			p.last_grounded_at = time_since_start
			p.grounded_time += dt
		} else {
			p.grounded_time = 0
		}

		if time_since(p.last_grounded_at) < 0.1 && input.jump {
			p.vel.y = -230
		}

		if input.separate {
			player_set_state(p, Player_State_Head_Detatching{})
			
			break
		}

		if input.nose {
			player_set_state(p, Player_State_Nose_Extending{})
			
			break
		}

	case Player_State_Head_Detatching:
		anim_length := animation_length(.Player_Detach_Head)
		camera_lerp_t := remap(time_since_start, d.state_start_time, d.state_start_time + f64(anim_length), 0, 1)
		set_camera_pos_x_offset(math.lerp(f32(0), dir_from_facing(p.facing).x * 8, f32(camera_lerp_t)))

		if animation_update(&p.anim, dt) {
			head := Entity {
				pos = p.pos + dir_from_facing(p.facing) * 8 - {0,3},
				facing = p.facing,
				tex = .Player_Head,
				collider = {1, 1, 6,6},
				origin = texture_middle(.Player_Head),
				type = .Player_Head,
			}

			head_handle := add_entity(world, head)
			set_controlled_entity(head_handle)
			set_camera_pos_x_offset(0)

			body := Entity {
				pos = p.pos,
				facing = p.facing,
				tex = .Player_Body,
				collider = {1, 1, 8,7},
				type = .Player_Body,
				origin = texture_bottom_middle(.Player_Body) - {0, 1},
			}

			add_entity(world, body)
			remove_entity(world, p.handle)
			return
		}

	case Player_State_Nose_Extending:
		dist := f32(state_elapsed) * 300

		if dist > 100 {
			player_set_state(p, Player_State_Nose_Retracting {
				tip_pos = s.tip_pos,
			})
			break
		}

		r := player_nose_tip_rect(s.tip_pos)

		if world_check_collision_SLOW(world, r, p, { .Player }) {
			player_set_state(p, Player_State_Nose_Retracting {
				tip_pos = s.tip_pos,
			})
			break
		}

		pos := player_mouth_pos(p^) + dir_from_facing(p.facing) * dist
		s.tip_pos = pos
		debug_draw_circle(pos, 1, k2.YELLOW)

	case Player_State_Nose_Retracting:
		tip_to_mouth := s.tip_pos - player_mouth_pos(p^)
		dir := linalg.normalize0(tip_to_mouth)
		s.tip_pos -= dir * dt * 300

		if linalg.length(tip_to_mouth) < 2 {
			player_set_state(p, Player_State_Default{})
		}

	case Player_State_Nose_Retracting_Pull:
		tip_to_mouth := s.tip_pos - player_mouth_pos(p^)
		dir := linalg.normalize0(tip_to_mouth)
		p.vel = dir * 300

		if coll, _ := entity_tick_physics_SLOW(p, { .Player }); coll || linalg.length(tip_to_mouth) < 2 {
			player_set_state(p, Player_State_Default{})
		}
	}
}

player_mouth_pos :: proc(p: Entity) -> Vec2 {
	return p.pos + {3 * dir_from_facing(p.facing).x, -12}
}

player_nose_tip_rect :: proc(pos: Vec2) -> Rect {
	return rect_from_pos_size(pos - { 1.5, 1.5 }, { 3,3 })
}

player_draw :: proc(p: Entity) {
	d := p.data.(Player_Data)

	switch s in d.state {
	case Player_State_Default:
		entity_draw(p)

	case Player_State_Head_Detatching:
		entity_draw(p)

	case Player_State_Nose_Extending:
		entity_draw(p)
		k2.draw_line(player_mouth_pos(p), s.tip_pos, 3, COLOR_BG)
		k2.draw_line(player_mouth_pos(p), s.tip_pos, 1, COLOR_FG)

	case Player_State_Nose_Retracting:
		entity_draw(p)
		k2.draw_line(player_mouth_pos(p), s.tip_pos, 3, COLOR_BG)
		k2.draw_line(player_mouth_pos(p), s.tip_pos, 1, COLOR_FG)

	case Player_State_Nose_Retracting_Pull:
		entity_draw(p)
		k2.draw_line(player_mouth_pos(p), s.tip_pos, 1, COLOR_FG)
	}
}

player_head_tick :: proc(p: ^Entity) {
	if input.movement_x != 0 {
		animation_update(&p.anim, dt)
	}

	if input.movement_x > 0 {
		p.facing = .East
	} else if input.movement_x < 0 {
		p.facing = .West
	}

	p.vel.x = input.movement_x * 80
	p.vel.y += 1000 * dt
	p.rot += p.vel.x * math.TAU * dt
	_, grounded := entity_tick_physics_SLOW(p, { .Player })

	if body := find_entity_by_type(world, .Player_Body); body != nil {
		above_body_rect := entity_collider(body^)
		above_body_rect.y -= 7
		debug_draw_rect(above_body_rect, COLOR_DEBUG_GREEN)

		if is_rect_intersecting(entity_collider(p^), above_body_rect) {
			set_controlled_entity(add_entity(world, entity_create_from_type(.Player, body.pos)))
			remove_entity(world, body.handle)
			remove_entity(world, p.handle)
			return
		}
	}

	if grounded {
		p.last_grounded_at = time_since_start
		p.grounded_time += dt
	} else {
		p.grounded_time = 0
	}

	if time_since(p.last_grounded_at) < 0.1 && input.jump {
		p.vel.y = -230
	}
}


================================================
File: game/rect.odin

// procs for modifying and managing rects

package game

import k2 "proj:karl2d"

Rect :: k2.Rect

RECT_EMPTY :: Rect{}

split_rect_top :: proc(r: Rect, y: f32, m: f32) -> (top, bottom: Rect) {
	top = r
	bottom = r
	top.y += m
	top.h = y
	bottom.y += y + m
	bottom.h -= y + m
	return
}

split_rect_left :: proc(r: Rect, x: f32, m: f32) -> (left, right: Rect) {
	left = r
	right = r
	left.w = x
	right.x += x + m
	right.w -= x +m
	return
}

split_rect_bottom :: proc(r: Rect, y: f32, m: f32) -> (top, bottom: Rect) {
	top = r
	top.h -= y + m
	bottom = r
	bottom.y = top.y + top.h + m
	bottom.h = y
	return
}

split_rect_right :: proc(r: Rect, x: f32, m: f32) -> (left, right: Rect) {
	left = r
	right = r
	right.w = x
	left.w -= x + m
	right.x = left.x + left.w
	return
}

cut_rect_top :: proc(r: ^Rect, y: f32, m: f32) -> Rect {
	res := r^
	res.y += m
	res.h = y
	r.y += y + m
	r.h -= y + m
	return res
}

cut_rect_bottom :: proc(r: ^Rect, h: f32, m: f32) -> Rect {
	res := r^
	res.h = h
	res.y = r.y + r.h - h - m
	r.h -= h + m
	return res
}

cut_rect_left :: proc(r: ^Rect, x, m: f32) -> Rect {
	res := r^
	res.x += m
	res.w = x
	r.x += x + m
	r.w -= x + m
	return res
}

cut_rect_right :: proc(r: ^Rect, w, m: f32) -> Rect {
	res := r^
	res.w = w
	res.x = r.x + r.w - w - m
	r.w -= w + m
	return res
}

rect_middle :: proc(r: Rect) -> Vec2 {
	return {
		r.x + r.w * 0.5,
		r.y + r.h * 0.5,
	}
}

rect_local_middle :: proc(r: Rect) -> Vec2 {
	return {
		r.w * 0.5,
		r.h * 0.5,
	}
}

inset_rect :: proc(r: Rect, x: f32, y: f32) -> Rect {
	return {
		r.x + x,
		r.y + y,
		r.w - x * 2,
		r.h - y * 2,
	}
}

rect_add_pos :: proc(r: Rect, p: Vec2) -> Rect {
	return {
		r.x + p.x,
		r.y + p.y,
		r.w,
		r.h,
	}
}

mouse_in_rect :: proc(r: Rect) -> bool {
	return point_in_rect(k2.get_mouse_position(), r)
}

mouse_in_world_rect :: proc(r: Rect, camera: k2.Camera) -> bool {
	return point_in_rect(k2.screen_to_world(k2.get_mouse_position(), camera), r)
}

rect_intersect :: proc(r1, r2: Rect) -> Rect {
	x1 := max(r1.x, r2.x)
	y1 := max(r1.y, r2.y)
	x2 := min(r1.x + r1.w, r2.x + r2.w)
	y2 := min(r1.y + r1.h, r2.y + r2.h)
	res: Rect
	
	if x1 < x2 && y1 < y2 {
		res = {
			x1,
			y1,
			x2 - x1,
			y2 - y1,
		}
	}

	return res
}

is_rect_intersecting :: proc(r1: Rect, r2: Rect) -> bool {
	return r1.x < r2.x + r2.w &&
		r1.x + r1.w > r2.x &&
		r1.y < r2.y + r2.h &&
		r1.y + r1.h > r2.y
}

point_in_rect :: proc(p: Vec2, r: Rect) -> bool {
	return p.x > r.x &&
		p.x < r.x + r.w &&
		p.y > r.y &&
		p.y < r.y + r.h
}

rect_from_pos_size :: proc(p: Vec2, s: Vec2) -> Rect {
	return {
		p.x, p.y,
		s.x, s.y,
	}
}

pos_from_rect :: proc(r: Rect) -> Vec2 {
	return {r.x, r.y}
}

size_from_rect :: proc(r: Rect) -> Vec2 {
	return {abs(r.w), abs(r.h)}
}

rect_is_empty :: proc(r: Rect) -> bool {
	return r.w == 0 || r.h == 0
}

File: game/tile.odin

package game

import k2 "proj:karl2d"

TILE_SIZE :: 8

Tile :: struct {
	pos: Vec2i,
	tile: Tile_Id,
	flip_x: bool,
	flip_y: bool,
}

tile_rect :: proc(p: Vec2i) -> Rect {
	wp := Vec2 {
		f32(p.x)*TILE_SIZE,
		f32(p.y)*TILE_SIZE,
	}

	return {
		wp.x, wp.y,
		TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE,
	}
}

tile_draw :: proc(t: Tile) {
	tile_world_pos := Vec2 {
		f32(t.pos.x) * TILE_SIZE,
		f32(t.pos.y) * TILE_SIZE,
	}

	atlas_rect := atlas_tiles[t.tile]

	if t.flip_x {
		atlas_rect.w *= -1
	}

	if t.flip_y {
		atlas_rect.h *= -1
	}

	k2.draw_texture_rect(atlas, atlas_rect, tile_world_pos)
}


================================================
File: game/world.odin

package game

import "core:slice"

World :: struct {
	entities: Handle_Array(Entity, Entity_Handle),
	tiles: []Tile,
	colliders: Handle_Array(Collider, Collider_Handle),
}

add_entity :: proc(w: ^World, e: Entity) -> Entity_Handle {
	return ha_add(&w.entities, e)
}

remove_entity :: proc(w: ^World, h: Entity_Handle) {
	ha_remove(&w.entities, h)
}

get_entity :: proc(w: ^World,  h: Entity_Handle) -> (Entity, bool ) {
	return ha_get(w.entities, h)
}

get_entity_ptr :: proc(w: ^World,  h: Entity_Handle) -> (^Entity, bool) #optional_ok {
	p := ha_get_ptr(w.entities, h)
	return p, p != nil
}

delete_world :: proc(w: World) {
	ha_delete(w.entities)
	ha_delete(w.colliders)
	delete(w.tiles)
}

clone_world :: proc(w: World, allocator := context.allocator) -> World {
	return {
		entities = ha_clone(w.entities, allocator),
		tiles = slice.clone(w.tiles, allocator),
		colliders = ha_clone(w.colliders, allocator),
	}
}

find_entity_by_type :: proc(w: ^World, et: Entity_Type) -> (^Entity, bool) #optional_ok {
	iter := ha_make_iter(&w.entities)
	for e in ha_iter_ptr(&iter) {
		if e.type == et {
			return e, true
		}
	}

	return nil, false
}

find_entity_handle_by_type :: proc(w: ^World, et: Entity_Type) -> Entity_Handle {
	iter := ha_make_iter(&w.entities)
	for e, h in ha_iter_ptr(&iter) {
		if e.type == et {
			return h
		}
	}

	return ENTITY_NONE
}

world_from_world_data :: proc(wd: World_Data, allocator := context.allocator) -> World {
	w := World {
		tiles = slice.clone(wd.tiles, allocator),

		entities = {
			allocator = allocator,
		},

		colliders = {
			allocator = allocator,
		},
	}

	for t in wd.tiles {
		ha_add(&w.colliders, Collider {
			rect = tile_rect(t.pos),
			layers = { .Player },
		})
	}

	for pet in wd.entities {
		e := entity_create_from_type(pet.type, pet.pos)
		h := add_entity(&w, e)

		if e.collider != RECT_EMPTY {
			ha_add(&w.colliders, Collider {
				entity = h,
				layers = { .Player },
			})
		}
	}

	ha_commit_new(&w.entities)
	ha_commit_new(&w.colliders)

	return w
}

world_check_collision_SLOW :: proc(w: ^World, col: Rect, col_owner: ^Entity, layers: bit_set[Collision_Layer]) -> bool {
	coll_iter := ha_make_iter(&w.colliders)

	for c in ha_iter_ptr(&coll_iter) {
		m := c.layers & layers

		if m != {} {
			if e := get_entity_ptr(w, c.entity); e != nil {
				if e.collider != RECT_EMPTY && is_rect_intersecting(col, rect_add_pos(e.collider, e.pos)) {
					entity_on_collision(e, col_owner)
					return true
				}
			}

			if c.rect != RECT_EMPTY && is_rect_intersecting(col, c.rect) {
				return true
			}
		}
	}

	return false
}

File: game/world_data.odin

package game

import "core:encoding/json"
import "core:os"
import "core:log"

/*Entity_Instance_Data_Door :: struct {
}

Entity_Instance_Data_Button :: struct {
	target: string,
}

Entity_Instance_Data :: union {
	Entity_Instance_Data_Door,
	Entity_Instance_Data_Button,
}*/

Entity_Data_Handle :: distinct Handle

Entity_Data :: struct {
	handle: Entity_Data_Handle,
	id: Entity_Id,
	type: Entity_Type,
	pos: Vec2,
	//instance_data: Entity_Instance_Data,
}

World_Data :: struct {
	entity_id_counter: Entity_Id,
	tiles: []Tile,
	entities: []Entity_Data,
}

world_data_save :: proc(filename: string, wd: World_Data) -> (res: bool) {
	json_opts := json.Marshal_Options {
		pretty = true,
		spec = .Bitsquid,
	}

	if bytes, err := json.marshal(wd, json_opts, context.temp_allocator); err == nil {
		if !os.write_entire_file(filename, bytes) {
			log.error("Failed writing world.json")
			res = false
		}
	} else {
		log.error(err)
		res = false
	}

	return
}

world_data_load :: proc(filename: string, allocator := context.allocator) -> (World_Data, bool) {
	wd: World_Data

	if data, ok := os.read_entire_file(filename, context.temp_allocator); ok {
		if err := json.unmarshal(data, &wd, .Bitsquid, allocator); err == nil {
			return wd, true
		} else {
			log.error(err)
			return {}, false
		}
	}

	log.error("Failed loading world data")
	return {}, false
}


================================================
File: main_hot_reload/main_hot_reload.odin
================================================
// Development game exe. Loads game.dll and reloads it whenever it changes.

package main

import "core:dynlib"
import "core:fmt"
import "core:c/libc"
import "core:os"
import "core:log"
import "core:mem"

when ODIN_OS == .Windows {
	DLL_EXT :: ".dll"
} else when ODIN_OS == .Darwin {
	DLL_EXT :: ".dylib"
} else {
	DLL_EXT :: ".so"
}

copy_dll :: proc(to: string) -> bool {
	exit: i32
	when ODIN_OS == .Windows {
		exit = libc.system(fmt.ctprintf("copy game.dll {0}", to))
	} else {
		exit = libc.system(fmt.ctprintf("cp game" + DLL_EXT + " {0}", to))
	}

	if exit != 0 {
		log.errorf("Failed to copy game" + DLL_EXT + " to {0}", to)
		return false
	}

	return true
}

Game_API :: struct {
	lib: dynlib.Library,
	init_window: proc(),
	init: proc(),
	update: proc() -> bool,
	shutdown: proc(),
	shutdown_window: proc(),
	memory: proc() -> rawptr,
	memory_size: proc() -> int,
	hot_reloaded: proc(mem: rawptr),
	force_reload: proc() -> bool,
	force_restart: proc() -> bool,
	modification_time: os.File_Time,
	api_version: int,
}

load_game_api :: proc(api_version: int) -> (api: Game_API, ok: bool) {
	mod_time, mod_time_error := os.last_write_time_by_name("game" + DLL_EXT)
	if mod_time_error != os.ERROR_NONE {
		log.errorf(
			"Failed getting last write time of game" + DLL_EXT + ", error code: {1}",
			mod_time_error,
		)
		return
	}

	// NOTE: this needs to be a relative path for Linux to work.
	game_dll_name := fmt.tprintf("{0}game_{1}" + DLL_EXT, "./" when ODIN_OS != .Windows else "", api_version)
	copy_dll(game_dll_name) or_return

	_, ok = dynlib.initialize_symbols(&api, game_dll_name, "game_", "lib")
	if !ok {
		log.panicf("Failed initializing symbols: {0}", dynlib.last_error())
	}

	api.api_version = api_version
	api.modification_time = mod_time
	ok = true

	return
}

unload_game_api :: proc(api: ^Game_API) {
	if api.lib != nil {
		if !dynlib.unload_library(api.lib) {
			log.errorf("Failed unloading lib: {0}", dynlib.last_error())
		}
	}

	if os.remove(fmt.tprintf("game_{0}" + DLL_EXT, api.api_version)) != nil {
		log.errorf("Failed to remove game_{0}" + DLL_EXT + " copy", api.api_version)
	}
}

main :: proc() {
	context.logger = log.create_console_logger()

	default_allocator := context.allocator
	tracking_allocator: mem.Tracking_Allocator
	mem.tracking_allocator_init(&tracking_allocator, default_allocator)
	context.allocator = mem.tracking_allocator(&tracking_allocator)

	reset_tracking_allocator :: proc(a: ^mem.Tracking_Allocator) -> bool {
		err := false

		for _, value in a.allocation_map {
			log.errorf("%v: Leaked %v bytes", value.location, value.size)
			err = true
		}

		mem.tracking_allocator_clear(a)
		return err
	}

	game_api_version := 0
	game_api, game_api_ok := load_game_api(game_api_version)

	if !game_api_ok {
		log.error("Failed to load Game API")
		return
	}

	game_api_version += 1
	game_api.init_window()
	game_api.init()

	old_game_apis := make([dynamic]Game_API, default_allocator)

	window_open := true
	for window_open {
		window_open = game_api.update()
		force_reload := game_api.force_reload()
		force_restart := game_api.force_restart()
		reload := force_reload || force_restart
		game_dll_mod, game_dll_mod_err := os.last_write_time_by_name("game" + DLL_EXT)

		if game_dll_mod_err == os.ERROR_NONE && game_api.modification_time != game_dll_mod {
			reload = true
		}

		if reload {
			new_game_api, new_game_api_ok := load_game_api(game_api_version)

			if new_game_api_ok {
				if game_api.memory_size() != new_game_api.memory_size() || force_restart {
					game_api.shutdown()
					reset_tracking_allocator(&tracking_allocator)

					for &g in old_game_apis {
						unload_game_api(&g)
					}

					clear(&old_game_apis)
					unload_game_api(&game_api)
					game_api = new_game_api
					game_api.init()
				} else {
					append(&old_game_apis, game_api)
					game_memory := game_api.memory()
					game_api = new_game_api
					game_api.hot_reloaded(game_memory)
				}

				game_api_version += 1
			}
		}

		if len(tracking_allocator.bad_free_array) > 0 {
			for b in tracking_allocator.bad_free_array {
				log.errorf("Bad free at: %v", b.location)
			}

			libc.getchar()
			panic("Bad free detected")
		}

		free_all(context.temp_allocator)
	}

	free_all(context.temp_allocator)
	game_api.shutdown()
	game_api.shutdown_window()
	
	if reset_tracking_allocator(&tracking_allocator) {
		libc.getchar()
	}

	for &g in old_game_apis {
		unload_game_api(&g)
	}

	delete(old_game_apis)

	unload_game_api(&game_api)
	mem.tracking_allocator_destroy(&tracking_allocator)
}

// make game use good GPU on laptops etc

@(export)
NvOptimusEnablement: u32 = 1

@(export)
AmdPowerXpressRequestHighPerformance: i32 = 1



================================================
File: main_release/main_release.odin
================================================
// For making a release exe that does not use hot reload.

package main_release

import "core:log"
import "core:os"

import game ".."

USE_TRACKING_ALLOCATOR :: #config(USE_TRACKING_ALLOCATOR, false)

main :: proc() {
	when USE_TRACKING_ALLOCATOR {
		default_allocator := context.allocator
		tracking_allocator: Tracking_Allocator
		tracking_allocator_init(&tracking_allocator, default_allocator)
		context.allocator = allocator_from_tracking_allocator(&tracking_allocator)
	}

	mode: int = 0
	when ODIN_OS == .Linux || ODIN_OS == .Darwin {
		mode = os.S_IRUSR | os.S_IWUSR | os.S_IRGRP | os.S_IROTH
	}

	logh, logh_err := os.open("log.txt", (os.O_CREATE | os.O_TRUNC | os.O_RDWR), mode)

	if logh_err == os.ERROR_NONE {
		os.stdout = logh
		os.stderr = logh
	}

	logger := logh_err == os.ERROR_NONE ? log.create_file_logger(logh) : log.create_console_logger()
	context.logger = logger
	
	game.game_init_window()
	game.game_init()

	window_open := true
	for window_open {
		window_open = game.game_update()

		when USE_TRACKING_ALLOCATOR {
			for b in tracking_allocator.bad_free_array {
				log.error("Bad free at: %v", b.location)
			}

			clear(&tracking_allocator.bad_free_array)
		}

		free_all(context.temp_allocator)
	}

	free_all(context.temp_allocator)
	game.game_shutdown()
	game.game_shutdown_window()
	
	if logh_err == os.ERROR_NONE {
		log.destroy_file_logger(logger)
	}

	when USE_TRACKING_ALLOCATOR {
		for key, value in tracking_allocator.allocation_map {
			log.error("%v: Leaked %v bytes\n", value.location, value.size)
		}

		tracking_allocator_destroy(&tracking_allocator)
	}
}

// make game use good GPU on laptops etc

@(export)
NvOptimusEnablement: u32 = 1

@(export)
AmdPowerXpressRequestHighPerformance: i32 = 1
